# guard-config-census — the config-census leg, v3.1 codified (recreation + practice)

_Built 2026-11-15 on HQ's charge (owed checks, 2026-11-15 ~08:02 EST), codifying candidate v3.1 from recreation Cycle-008 Wave-6's Condense (which extended Wave-5's v3, which extended v2). Before this build the census existed only as candidacy text inside RUN records and as builder-run greps whose blind spots the scoring lenses kept finding. This file is the leg's standing definition. It owns ONE defect class: reusability failures whose mechanism is **config incompleteness** — shaping decisions living outside CONFIG, or CONFIG keys that promise control they do not exert (reusability stuck across Waves 3–6 and binding in practice waves since). It does NOT own timing, fit (guard-measured-fit's class), or content failures._

## What the leg checks (per item, on the final recreation.html)
1. **All-literal scan.** Every literal outside the CONFIG block is counted and classified: hex/rgba colors · durations · geometry values · numeric constants and formula coefficients. Each constant is classified in print as **subject-shaping** (it shapes what the subject looks like or how it moves → must be a CONFIG key; standing outside = HIT) or **structural** (loop bounds, indices, mathematical identities → exempt, classification printed per constant; the sr/3, sr/6 misclassification of Wave 6 is the cautionary instance — ratios that scale the subject are subject-shaping). **Addendum landed 2026-11-16 (recreation Cycle 024 Condense — the A9.6 background-coefficient clause, discharging Cycle 023's named consumer):** coefficients that set a BACKGROUND layer's growth, opacity, size, or intensity AGAINST the subject (dust-cloud growth/opacity, plate glow, reserve-field density/intensity, backdrop scale) are **subject-shaping** whenever a scoring lens can price them against the subject — a background layer's coefficients are subject-shaping when the readability or reusability lens can price them, and they must be CONFIG keys or be classified HIT; classifying them "structural" because they sit in a background generator is the named miss. Forcing evidence: T140 (Cycle 023) — the builder's census classified the dust coefficients structural while the reusability lens priced them subject-shaping at 78, the item's minimum (filed return: `hidden_files/study/recreations/T140-repetition-chant/codex/lens-reusability.md`, SCORE: 78, seat-verified at Cycle 024's Condense); INSTRUCTION Amendment A9.6 resolved the classification in the instruction layer the same cycle, and this addendum lands the same rule in the guard's own classification text. **Clarification landed 2026-11-15 (recreation Cycle 013 Condense, on T75's reusability-lens miss):** constants inside scene/asset GENERATORS that scale or shape the generated subject (skyline/scene geometry, generator seeds' geometric coefficients) are subject-shaping, not structural, whenever changing them changes what the subject looks like; font-family stacks named in CSS are likewise subject-shaping (typography is a shaping decision) and must be CONFIG keys or be classified HIT — a census that passes either class as "structural" has missed its own class. **Addendum landed 2026-11-16 (recreation Cycle 021 Condense, under codex-condense REVISE — invariant-exemption bar):** the declared-invariant classification is barred for any literal that implements a dimension a CONFIG key promises — if a geom.* key names it, the literals constructing it are subject-shaping and must read from the key. Forcing evidence: T130 (Cycle 021) — the car silhouette path coordinates and wheel offsets were exempted as declared invariants while geom.carW/carH promised that control; reusability lens 68.
2. **Application-site resolution.** Every CONFIG key is resolved to at least one application site in the code, **including nested and array-member keys** (`artworkTags[].level` class — Wave 6, T43). A key with no application site is a HIT (unapplied key = promised control that does not exist). **Addendum landed 2026-11-16 (recreation Cycle 021 Condense, under codex-condense REVISE — value-flow site validity):** an application site counts only where the key's value flows into the construction of the subject the key names (e.g. carW/carH → the car body's path/transform and wheel geometry). A key whose only occurrences are its declaration, storage, or a consumer other than its named subject (e.g. carW read only by a puff-midpoint computation) is UNAPPLIED = HIT. Forcing evidence: T130 (Cycle 021) — census HITs 0 with "geom.carW/carH → car scale declaration" while carH occurred only in its declaration and the car path/wheels were hardcoded; reusability lens 68.
3. **CSS-variable setter check.** A CONFIG key consumed through a CSS variable whose setter never assigns it is a HIT (the `geom.pieSize` false-negative class, Wave 6 T38: a reference, not a literal — invisible to literal greps, owned here explicitly).

## Output form (filed per item, pre-wave)
`config-census.md` in the item's artifact directory: literal count by class with the per-constant classification, the key→site resolution table, the CSS-variable check, and a HIT list (empty = PASS for this leg). The wave's RUN cites the path + hit count per item. The leg's verdict is ADVISORY (owner's 2026-11-14 ruling): hits are cured before scoring where the builder can, or carried into the wave printed with their disposition. The FIRING is not advisory: a wave whose record lacks the census for any item prints INCOMPLETE.

## Call point
Pre-wave, per item, after the build and BEFORE the five-lens scoring wave (wired into recreation INSTRUCTION Amendment A6 and practice INSTRUCTION Amendment A-C008). Practice re-attempts fire the same leg on the new attempt's file; the census runs against the attempt's own CONFIG, never the prior attempt's.

## Scope honesty
This leg proves the recreation is re-drivable from its CONFIG — every shaping decision named, every named key actually applied. It does not prove the CONFIG values are well chosen, the swap is beautiful, or the timing is right; the scoring wave prices those.

## Firing record
- 2026-11-15: BUILT (this file). First firing owed: the next recreation wave + the next practice wave, unconditionally.
- 2026-11-15 (recreation Cycle 013 Wave 11): FIRST FIRING ×5 items (with guard-measured-fit) — final HIT lists empty ×5 after pre-cure cures; one MISS recorded (T75: scene-generator constants + CSS font stacks passed as structural; the lens priced it) and cured by the classification clarification landed in §1 above the same Condense.
- 2026-11-16 (recreation Cycle 021 Wave 18): firing ×6 items; one MISS recorded (T130: declaration accepted as application site + car literals exempted as declared invariants; reusability lens 68 priced it) and cured the same Condense by the §1 invariant-exemption bar + §2 value-flow site-validity addenda above, re-encoded under codex-condense REVISE (the drafted site-category cure was rejected as amendment-theatre — the operative test is what counts as a site).
# guard-measured-fit — the measured-fit leg (recreation + practice)

_Built 2026-11-15 on HQ's charge (owed checks, 2026-11-15 ~08:02 EST), from the candidate spec filed in recreation Cycle-007 Wave-5's Condense and applied ad hoc since. This file is the leg's standing definition; before this build the leg existed only as candidacy text inside RUN records (Waves 5–6) and as a builder-side instrument — never as a guard with a call point. It owns ONE defect class: readability failures whose mechanism is **fit asserted, not measured** (readability stuck/under-floor in Waves 2, 3, 4, 5 and again in Wave 10 — T54 density, T57 attribution typography). It does NOT own timing anchors, density judgment, or any content-level failure; those classes stay with their own guards._

## What the leg checks (per item, on the final recreation.html)
For EVERY text element that carries subject content (titles, labels, captions-in-scene, cards, marquees, counters):
1. **A measurement row exists, with provenance.** The item's build record contains a fit table with one row per subject text element: element id · container (box/strip/line) · the INSTRUMENT (harness run or rendered-measurement tool that produced the row) · the fitted element + stage geometry · MEASURED ink bounds after fitting (glyph ink height and width, from post-fit measurement of the rendered geometry at the 360 px stage) · container bounds · margin on the binding side. A text element with no row, or a row missing its measurement provenance, is a HIT. *(Provenance form added 2026-11-18, recreation Cycle 035 Condense — Amendment A12's paired landing: T196 + T197's model rows.)*
2. **Fit is measured, never formula-claimed.** A row whose bounds are derived from the fitting formula's own assertion (font size × assumed factor — a character-width × factor MODEL) rather than from a post-fit measurement of the rendered geometry is a HIT — the formula is the claim, not the evidence (founding instances: T20's glyph-height overstatement, ink at 76% of claimed cell pitch; T19's floored 11 px phrase in a box that could not hold it; T57's attribution lines). In a seat with no browser binary, the lawful measurements are (i) harness post-fit ink measurement of the built spans, or (ii) rendered measurement with a NAMED stand-in font, the row labeled PROXY with the font named (demonstrations: T195 harness post-fit · T190 PIL rendered measurement, Cycle 035).
3. **Legibility floor.** Measured subject-text ink height below ~11 px at the 360 px stage width is a HIT, whatever the formula claimed.
4. **Hierarchy is measured too.** Where the design claims an intensity/size hierarchy between neighbouring text elements, the rows must show the measured ratio; a hue-only hierarchy against near-equal measured sizes is a HIT (T20's neighbour-window failure).

## Output form (filed per item, pre-wave)
`fit-table.md` in the item's artifact directory: the full row set (each row carrying its instrument/provenance per check 1; stand-in rows labeled PROXY with the font named) + a HIT list (empty = PASS for this leg). The wave's RUN cites the table's path + hit count per item. The leg's verdict is ADVISORY (owner's 2026-11-14 ruling): hits are priced into the build before scoring where the builder can cure them; hits carried into the scoring wave are printed in the RUN with their disposition. What is NOT advisory is the firing: a wave whose record lacks the fit table for any item prints INCOMPLETE.

## Call point
Pre-wave, per item, after the build and BEFORE the five-lens scoring wave (wired into recreation INSTRUCTION Amendment A6 and practice INSTRUCTION Amendment A-C008 — the legs run before scoring, not after). Practice re-attempts fire the same leg on the new attempt's file.

## Scope honesty
This leg proves text fits its container at phone width, measured. It does not prove the text is the right text, the right density, or readable in motion — timing, density judgment, and content stay with the scoring wave and their own guards.

## Firing record
- 2026-11-15: BUILT (this file). First firing owed: the next recreation wave + the next practice wave, unconditionally.
# Guards — recreation

## The judgment layer's home (owner's order, 2026-11-14 — 100% form)
Form: **In-job form.** This job's judging = the Codex scoring wave (its review type, defined in `codex-scoring-wave.md` in this directory) + the mechanical guards below + `jev-prewave.md`. The wave's execution may be shared tooling; the definitions are this job's own files.

## The commands (mechanical guards — callable, demonstrated)
1. Construction-purity grep — recreation.html must contain no reference to source media, frames, or tracing (pattern census over the file).
   Demonstrated: 2026-11-13, T15 attempt 1 (zero hits; Codex purity lens independently returned 100).
   Pattern precision (2026-11-17, Cycle 026 Condense — priced across T167 · T170 · T175 in one wave): an in-file fragment reference `url(#id)` (an SVG paint-server or clip definition inside the same file) is NOT a specimen reference; such hits are classified in the item's build record and never counted as purity hits. The refusal class is specimen media, source files, and tracing — the grep's specimen alternatives (`segment.mp4|source.mp4|<video|<img|1235931636`-form source ids) stay unmodified.
2. Swap test (parameterization proof) — headless harness loads the recreation with a swapped CONFIG phrase; target geometry must regenerate for the new words (a traced copy cannot).
   Demonstrated: 2026-11-13, T15 attempt 1, LOGIC level (node harness, stubbed canvas: 5,060 → 2,728 targets). Pixel-level render proof is NOT exercisable from this seat (no browser binary) — printed limitation, carried in scores.md; the comparison page is the render surface.
3. Syntax + determinism check — `node --check` on inline scripts; seeded-RNG presence census.
   Demonstrated: 2026-11-13, T15 attempt 1 (both scripts clean).
4. Scores arithmetic — recompute the wave average + min from the filed lens returns before scores.md prints a verdict.
   Demonstrated: 2026-11-13, T15 attempt 1 (68/82/100/64 → 78.5 recomputed at filing).
5. Segment binding — segment.mp4's timestamps/duration/ffprobe facts recorded against the packet's technique window before any build starts.
   Demonstrated: 2026-11-13, T15 attempt 1 (1:24–1:40 · 16.0 s · ffprobe-cited).
6. **Measured-fit leg** — `guard-measured-fit.md` in this directory. Per item, pre-wave: every subject text element carries a MEASURED fit row (post-fit ink bounds vs container at the 360 px stage, legibility floor ~11 px); a formula-claimed fit or a missing row is a HIT. Owns the "fit asserted, not measured" readability class (Waves 2–5, 10). **Built 2026-11-15 (HQ owed-checks charge); fires unconditionally at the pre-wave step from the next cycle; a wave record lacking a fit table for any item prints INCOMPLETE.** Verdict advisory; firing is not.
7. **Config-census leg (v3.1 codified)** — `guard-config-census.md` in this directory. Per item, pre-wave: all-literal scan with per-constant subject-shaping/structural classification + CONFIG key→application-site resolution (nested/array keys included) + CSS-variable setter check; unapplied keys and unset-variable references are HITs. Owns the config-incompleteness reusability class. **Built 2026-11-15 (HQ owed-checks charge); fires unconditionally at the pre-wave step from the next cycle; a wave record lacking a census for any item prints INCOMPLETE.** Verdict advisory; firing is not.

Call point: INSTRUCTION §2 gate out. **Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle. A cycle in which it did not fire prints INCOMPLETE.** (2026-11-14.)
- Deliverable Jev: **none separate, by design** — the deliverable class (a scored recreation attempt) is judged by the five-lens Codex scoring wave + §5.5 consolidation; a jev-verify file would duplicate the wave. jev-prewave is its pre-wave gate. (Printed in INSTRUCTION §5 step 3b.)

# Jev call — jev-observe-exit (recreation)

_Per-call definition from jev-call-template.md. Built 2026-11-14 (phase-guard augmentation). The judgment: a recreation wave may not enter Plan on a catalogue summary alone — Observe must have priced the queue, read the prior scores where they exist, seen the evidence frames, and carried the standing construction guidance into working memory._

## 1. What it judges
- Deliverable: the wave's Observe working memory — RUN.md gate-out block (§1: mechanics summary + sub-beat timings + variable list + named consumer) for each item in the wave (up to 5 owed items).
- Call point: recreation INSTRUCTION §1, at the gate out of Observe, before §2 Plan opens for any item in the wave. The verdict is advisory per item: applied, a RETURN-TO-OBSERVE item is held out of the wave's Plan (the wave proceeds with its advancing items; a held item never holds the wave hostage — same rule as jev-prewave §5); set aside, the miss is recorded in Condense. An unfired call makes the cycle INCOMPLETE.

## 2. Instruction (the model's charge)
Judge whether this Observe record can support a Plan for THIS recreation wave, item by item. Price, in order:
(1) QUEUE STATE PRICED — does the record state the recreation queue's current position (../QUEUE.md read this cycle), each item's queue number and OWED status, and the ownership split (Amendment A3 §3: this job owns FIRST attempts; an already-scored technique belongs to practice — a wave item that is a re-attempt is a routing defect, named)? T15 is the standing example: attempt 1 scored FAIL 78.5 on 2026-11-13, so T15 attempt 2 is practice's charge, never this job's wave item.
(2) PRIOR SCORES READ — for any item whose technique already has a scores.md or a verdict-history line (a techniques' family sibling counts: e.g. T16 shares T15's particle machinery), does the record cite the actual filed scores and cure list, or does it plan from the catalogue description alone? Planning that ignores a filed failure is the defect this leg exists to catch.
(3) MECHANICS SEEN, NOT SUMMARIZED — does the record carry the packet section's mechanics + sub-beat timings from firsthand viewing of the evidence frames and the source segment in motion (§1.1–1.2), with timings in seconds — or only a restatement of the TECHNIQUES.md one-liner? A mechanic the builder has not seen is a guess (§1.1).
(4) VARIABLES EXTRACTED — is the §1.3 variable list complete for this technique (text content, phrase count, timing/durations, palette, scale, direction, density — plus technique-specific variables), so Plan's parameter table has something to bind? A variable missing here becomes a hardcoded accident in Execute (§1.3).
(5) CONSTRUCTION GUIDANCE CARRIED — where the technique's family has standing guidance, is it IN the working memory, named? For particle-field techniques: the flow-field model (Amendment A5 §4: one constant flow field, borrowing with low-amplitude jitter, never-borrowed reserve fraction, release with velocity continuity) AND Owner Cure 1 (foreground/background intensity separation riding the borrow flag) AND Owner Cure 2 (strict phrase sequencing / phase exclusivity). For typography techniques: the typography construction precedent (Amendment A1 §2: runtime sampling of config-rendered text). Guidance that exists in INSTRUCTION but is absent from the wave's working memory is scored as absent.
(6) CONSUMER NAMED — §1.4: which production surface lifts this machinery (container type + owning lane), named per item, not "production generally."
(7) CODEX/OWNER INPUTS — were any Codex calls fired in this Observe (e.g. a record audit of a prior attempt's codex/ returns) with receipts filed, or is the absence printed? Does the wave need owner-only information (it almost never does — recreation prices from packets and filed scores); if claimed, is it routed as a named gap rather than silently assumed?
Score 0–1 per dimension (§4), classify defects from the taxonomy, return one verdict per item.

## 3. Context contract (masked view)
- FED: RUN.md Observe gate-out blocks · ../QUEUE.md recreation-queue section (current) · each item's packet digest.md technique section + TECHNIQUES.md entry · prior scores.md + verdict-history.md lines for the item and its named family siblings · INSTRUCTION Amendments A1/A3/A5 guidance lines relevant to the item's family · any Observe-phase Codex receipts filed this cycle.
- NEVER FED: the builder's confidence prose · HQ's supervision faces · scores from attempts not yet filed (unfiled scores do not exist).
- Ceiling: ≤32k tokens; packet sections enter as the technique's section only, never the whole digest; evidence frames are referenced by path/timestamp, never fed as images (text-only judge).

## 4. Output space
- Verdict classes (per item): **ADVANCE · ADVANCE-WITH-GAPS · RETURN-TO-OBSERVE**.
- Dimensions (0–1): queue-state-priced · prior-scores-read · mechanics-seen · variables-extracted · guidance-carried · consumer-named. (1.0 = evidenced in the record with citations; 0.5 = asserted without citation; 0.0 = absent.)
- Rejection taxonomy (one primary per returned item): QUEUE-STALE (queue position/status not read this cycle) · REATTEMPT-MISROUTED (scored technique in a first-attempt wave) · SCORES-UNREAD (prior scores/cures exist, uncited) · MECHANICS-UNSEEN (no firsthand sub-beat record) · VARIABLES-THIN (variable list cannot bind a parameter table) · GUIDANCE-DROPPED (standing family guidance absent from working memory) · CONSUMER-VAGUE.
- Accepted profiles travel: cited in RUN.md at the Plan gate; ADVANCE-WITH-GAPS items carry their gap list into §2 as named Plan charges.

## 5. Floors and disposition
- Any dimension < 0.5 → RETURN-TO-OBSERVE for that item. GUIDANCE-DROPPED on a particle-field or typography item → RETURN-TO-OBSERVE at any other score (T15 attempt 1's founding lesson: the frozen-flow defect and both owner catches were guidance-shaped failures discovered only after the wave was spent).
- ADVANCE-WITH-GAPS requires every gap named with its Plan-phase cure; an unnamed gap is a RETURN.
- Disposition: the verdict is advisory — reviewed by the runner, applied or set aside with the miss recorded in Condense. The verdict + primary classification + disposition land in RUN.md either way.

## Call command
- Defined prompt + context assembly per §3, executed through the fleet's sealed decision-model route named in guards.md when bound. Binding the exact runner command here is the first firing's own first step. **Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle. A cycle in which it did not fire prints INCOMPLETE.**

## 6. Firing record and improvement
- Exemplars: (a) T15 Cycle-002's Observe record reconstructed from its RUN.md (must ADVANCE — mechanics, timings and consumer were recorded before building). (b) A constructed record proposing T15 as a wave item for attempt 2 (must RETURN-TO-OBSERVE as REATTEMPT-MISROUTED). (c) A constructed particle-technique record with no flow-field / Owner Cure 1 / Owner Cure 2 lines (must RETURN as GUIDANCE-DROPPED). (d) A constructed record whose variable list names only "text and color" for a timed multi-phrase technique (must RETURN as VARIABLES-THIN).
- Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle; its firing record and misses are its improvement material. No test precedes firing. The exemplars above stand as its first firing cases — all four must classify correctly when fired, and a miss is recorded in Condense as improvement material, never as a bar to firing.

## 7. Iteration (Condense)
Amendments name forcing evidence + before/after. A text change takes effect on the call's next firing; the call keeps firing every cycle, and Condense prices the change against the returns that follow it. First expected amendment pressure: whether family-sibling scores (leg 2) should be mandatory for all items or only same-machinery families — priced from the first waves' returns.
# Jev call — jev-plan-exit (recreation)

_Per-call definition from jev-call-template.md. Built 2026-11-14 (phase-guard augmentation). The judgment: Execute is spent only on a Plan that already contains its checklists, its failure points, and its plateau exposure — priced in Plan, never discovered at close._

## 1. What it judges
- Deliverable: per-item Plan product in RUN.md (§2 gate out: parameter table + construction choice + verification plan) plus the wave manifest's per-item summaries.
- Call point: recreation INSTRUCTION §2, at the gate out of Plan, before §3 Execute opens for an item. The verdict is advisory per item — applied, or set aside with the miss recorded in Condense. An unfired call makes the cycle INCOMPLETE.

## 2. Instruction (the model's charge)
Judge whether this Plan can be executed and verified without improvisation, item by item. Price, in order:
(1) PARAMETER TABLE COMPLETE — every variable from the Observe gate-out (§1.3 list, as passed by jev-observe-exit) is a named config key with the specimen's value as default (§2.1). A variable with no key, or animation logic the Plan already describes with literals, is a Plan failure named here — not an Execute surprise.
(2) CONSTRUCTION CHOICE PRICED — the HTML-native construction is chosen for the technique's mechanics (canvas particle system · CSS keyframes · SVG draw-on · DOM animation · generated imagery + overlays) with rejected alternatives named in one line each (§2.2). For particle-field items the choice must instantiate the flow-field model (Amendment A5 §4); for typography items it must start from runtime text sampling (Amendment A1 §2) or name why not. Owner Cure 1 (intensity separation) and Owner Cure 2 (phase exclusivity) appear as construction requirements where the technique is a particle field or a sequenced-phrase piece — absent cures in a particle/sequence Plan = the T15 attempt-1 failure re-planned.
(3) EXECUTE CHECKLIST EXISTS — a step list for §3 (segment cut with timestamps ±0.5 s handles · recreation.html build · comparison.html build with synchronized controls + phone stacking · self-verify legs) concrete enough that a builder who did not write the Plan could execute it.
(4) VERIFY CHECKLIST EXISTS, AS INSTRUMENTS — §2.4's verification plan names: the swap test's exact altered values (words minimum + one timing + one palette value, §5.1) · the purity greps + asset inventory (§5.2/§6.1) · the comparison check (§5.3) · jev-prewave's call point (§5, between own checks and the wave) · the five-lens Codex wave composition (§5.4: motion fidelity · timing/tempo · construction purity · reusability · readability). A checklist item stated as an intention ("check purity") without its instrument (the grep patterns / harness) fails this leg.
(5) FAILURE POINTS EVALUATED — the Plan names what breaks it, per item: at minimum priced from this job's record — surplus-particle freeze (T15: motion 68 + reuse 64 convergent defect) · word-vs-reserve intensity collapse (owner catch 1, readability) · phrase-lifecycle overlap (owner catch 2) · fitting/wrapping limits for long words (reuse 64: fitting stops ~14 px, no multiline) · pool/density guards · the render-proof limitation (no browser binary in this seat — the comparison page is the render surface; the Plan must state how its absence is carried, as attempt 1's scores.md did). Technique-specific failure points beyond this floor are expected; a Plan whose failure list is only this floor, copied, is scored down on specificity.
(6) PLATEAU FLOOR PRICED IN PLAN — the floor rule (Amendment A2, practice §floor as close-out gate) is priced HERE: the Plan states the item's prior average where one exists in its family line or states FIRST-ATTEMPT (no prior), and names in advance what a non-beating result would mean (which lens is expected to be stuck, the hypothesized cause, the WHY the scores.md entry will carry). A re-attempt-shaped build (same machinery family, e.g. T16 on T15's pool) that reaches close-out and discovers its plateau there has failed this leg retroactively — the Condense record says so.
(7) REDUCTIONS NAMED — anything the Plan quietly drops from the packet's mechanics (a sub-beat deferred, a phase simplified) is named as a deferral with its reason (§2/jev-prewave fidelity-plan honesty); a silent deferral found here is classified, not absorbed.
Score 0–1 per dimension (§4), classify, return one verdict per item.

## 3. Context contract (masked view)
- FED: RUN.md Plan gate-out blocks + wave manifest · the Observe gate-out blocks with jev-observe-exit's filed verdicts · the item's scores.md / family scores.md cure lists where they exist · INSTRUCTION §2, §5, §6 + Amendments A1/A2/A5 · guards.md command list.
- NEVER FED: builder rationale prose about expected scores · prior waves' verdicts for unrelated techniques · any lens return not yet filed.
- Ceiling: ≤32k tokens; recreation.html does not exist yet at this gate and is never fed.

## 4. Output space
- Verdict classes (per item): **EXECUTE · REVISE · RETURN-TO-OBSERVE**.
- Dimensions (0–1): parameter-table-complete · construction-priced · execute-checklist-ready · verify-checklist-instrumented · failure-points-priced · plateau-priced.
- Rejection taxonomy (one primary): UNBOUND-VARIABLE · LITERAL-IN-LOGIC · CURE-ABSENT (Owner Cure 1/2 or family guidance missing from construction) · CHECKLIST-INTENTION (Verify item without instrument) · FAILURE-POINTS-GENERIC · PLATEAU-UNPRICED · SILENT-DEFERRAL · RETURN-TO-OBSERVE-ROOT (the Plan's defect is an Observe defect — mechanics/variables missing upstream).
- Accepted profiles travel: cited in RUN.md at the Execute gate; REVISE items carry their classified defect as the Plan amendment charge.

## 5. Floors and disposition
- verify-checklist-instrumented < 0.5 → REVISE (a Plan that cannot say how it will check itself does not execute). CURE-ABSENT on a particle-field or sequenced item → REVISE at any other score. PLATEAU-UNPRICED on a family re-use build → REVISE.
- RETURN-TO-OBSERVE is reserved for defects whose cure is upstream (missing mechanics/variables), and names the jev-observe-exit dimension that passed it — that citation is the observe-exit's firing-record evidence.
- Disposition: the verdict is advisory — reviewed by the runner, applied or set aside with the miss recorded in Condense. The verdict + disposition land in RUN.md either way.

## Call command
- Defined prompt + context assembly per §3, executed through the fleet's sealed decision-model route named in guards.md when bound. **Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle. A cycle in which it did not fire prints INCOMPLETE.**

## 6. Firing record and improvement
- Exemplars: (a) T15 attempt 1's Plan reconstructed from Cycle-002 RUN.md (must EXECUTE or REVISE only on named legs — it did carry a verification plan and a named interlude deferral). (b) A constructed particle Plan with no intensity-separation requirement (must REVISE as CURE-ABSENT). (c) A constructed Plan whose Verify checklist reads "run swap test, check purity, fire wave" with no altered values or grep patterns (must REVISE as CHECKLIST-INTENTION). (d) A constructed T16 Plan silent on T15's 78.5 and its stuck lenses (must REVISE as PLATEAU-UNPRICED).
- Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle; its firing record and misses are its improvement material. No test precedes firing. The exemplars above stand as its first firing cases — all four must classify correctly when fired, and a miss is recorded in Condense as improvement material, never as a bar to firing.

## 7. Iteration (Condense)
Amendments name forcing evidence + before/after. A text change takes effect on the call's next firing; the call keeps firing every cycle, and Condense prices the change against the returns that follow it. Standing watch: whether the §2.4 "four lenses" wording anywhere still survives against the five-lens form — any Plan citing four lenses is a FAILURE-POINTS-GENERIC signal, not a typo.

## Amendment — allocation/count consistency leg (2026-11-16, recreation Cycle 025 Condense, pattern promotion)
**The leg:** for every item whose Plan carries a schedule or allocation table, this gate prices the table's **census-anchor mapping** before Execute: each prescribed onset/duration is mapped into segment-relative time (window-start offset applied) and cross-checked against every census state the wave record names for that item. A prescribed value that contradicts a named census state at its instant (e.g. a front prescribed 8% grown at the census instant whose state reads "no territory"; arrivals prescribed complete at 11300 ms against a census anchor "crowded by ~7000 ms") is a REVISE charge, primary class **UNBOUND-SCHEDULE**. A Plan whose schedule table prints no census-anchor mapping is priced as carrying an unmapped schedule — the mapping's absence is itself the charge (the table cannot be checked, which is the defect's hiding place). Count assertions ride the same leg: a summary count in the Plan that disagrees with the table it summarizes is UNBOUND-SCHEDULE's count form (A9.3's gate side).
**Forcing evidence (three instances, one class):** T153 Wave 21 (timing 45 — §2b allocation contradicted the §1c census checkpoints it claimed to rest on) · T160 Wave 22 (timing 68 — prescribed onsets 2500/3500 ms already 8.33%/45.45% grown at the 449 s/452 s census instants) · T162 Wave 22 (timing 64 — even-stepped allocation completing at 11300 ms against census anchors at ~2000/~7000 ms). For T160 and T162 the build implemented the prescribed schedule exactly (verified from this cycle's filed returns, bound in Cycle 025 RUN §9b item 2); for T153 the same compliance is attributed to the seat's Wave-21 cycle record (Cycle 024 RUN §5) and is unverified in the Wave-22 stream. In all three the defect was the Plan's, discovered by the timing lens at the cost of the item's verdict.
**Before/after:** before — this gate priced parameter tables for completeness and checklists for instruments, but no leg owned schedule-vs-census consistency, so three instances across Waves 21–22 shipped census-contradicting schedules through a fired gate. After — the mapping is a named Plan product and this leg prices it at the gate, where the cure costs a table edit instead of a verdict.
# Jev call — jev-prewave (recreation)

_Per-call definition from jev-call-template.md. Designed 2026-11-14. The judgment: a scoring wave is the lane's expensive instrument — this call prices whether a build has EARNED its wave before the wave spends._

## 1. What it judges
- Deliverable: an attempt's build record (parameter table · construction route · own-checks results) BEFORE the Codex wave fires.
- Call point: recreation INSTRUCTION §4, between the builder's own checks (§5.2) and the wave (§5.3). The verdict is advisory: applied, a HOLD returns the build to the builder with named defects and the wave does not fire on that item; set aside, the miss is recorded in Condense. An unfired call makes the cycle INCOMPLETE.

## 2. Instruction (the model's charge)
Judge whether this build record shows a recreation ready to be scored, or one whose defects are already visible in its own paperwork. Price: (1) PARAMETERIZATION — is the piece genuinely CONFIG-driven (fields named, ranges sane), or hard-coded with a config costume? (2) PURITY PRE-CHECK — does the construction route describe generation from parameters, with no tracing/embedding step hiding in the prose? (3) OWN-CHECKS COMPLETENESS — are the mechanical guards' results recorded (purity grep, swap test, syntax), or asserted? **Census-checkpoint mapping (Amendment A11, landed 2026-11-17, Cycle 028 Condense): every Observe census checkpoint for the item must map to an executable own-check assertion whose evaluation instant is CONFIG-derived; the build-record identifies checkpoint · assertion · CONFIG expression · evaluated result. ASSERTED-CHECKS is returned if any checkpoint lacks this mapping, any evaluation instant uses hardcoded defaults, or execution evidence is absent; a CONFIG timing swap must be shown to move the corresponding assertion's evaluation instant. Receipt form (Amendment A12, landed 2026-11-18, Cycle 035 Condense): the execution evidence for each assertion is its EXECUTABLE RECEIPT — harness command or run · CONFIG expression evaluated · raw result from the build under review (for a timing swap, the receipt shows the changed evaluation instant); a claim without its receipt is execution evidence absent. **Receipt-index ground (Amendment A13, landed 2026-11-18, Cycle 036 Condense): a build-record whose RECEIPT INDEX (INSTRUCTION A13.1) is missing an expected entry (an applicable guards.md command, a planned A11 assertion, an assertion inherited whole from a prior attempt's filed harness, or a census row for an expected CONFIG key), carries an exclusion whose disposition cites no governing plan or guard, or carries a populated receipt lacking the evidence its assertion requires (CONFIG expression + evaluated value for CONFIG-derived inputs; raw observed result for checks) is an ASSERTED-CHECKS ground by name.** The same leg prices the A6 fit evidence: a fit-table row lacking measurement provenance, or carrying model-derived bounds, per guard-measured-fit.md checks 1–2 is an ASSERTED-CHECKS ground.** (4) TECHNIQUE FIDELITY PLAN — does the record state which packet mechanics the build implements and which it deliberately defers (a deferral named is lawful; a mechanic silently absent is not)? Score 0–1 per dimension, classify defects. You judge paperwork only: a build can pass you and still fail its wave — your job is that no wave is spent on a build its own record already condemns.

## 3. Context contract (masked view)
- FED: build-record.md · the CONFIG block of recreation.html · the technique's packet section · guards.md command list (what own-checks should exist) · **the item's A6 leg outputs (fit-table.md + config-census.md) — added 2026-11-16, recreation Cycle 024 Condense, on the Wave-21 pattern: prewave firings for T151, T153 and T154 all HOLDed or re-fired on feed completeness (the A6 files and packet section existed complete in the artifact directory but were not in the payload), the lane's recurring ASSERTED-CHECKS false-positive class. A payload lacking any FED element is a feeder defect, printed as such in the build record; a HOLD whose only named cure is feeding an already-filed FED element is disposed as a feed-completeness miss, with the miss recorded.**
- FED (added 2026-11-17, Cycle 026 Condense — the T167 miss): **the wave record's binding census-anchor mapping for the item + any Observe dispositions pricing source ambiguities** (the §1d-form: where a packet caption or digest line admits two readings, the wave record's priced reading governs). A HOLD founded on a source ambiguity the wave record has already priced is a feeder defect, disposed as such in the build record — the judge prices the build against the binding mapping, not against raw source prose. (Founding miss: Cycle 026 T167 — HOLD priced a packet caption's ambiguous reading against a build that followed the gate-approved mapping; the seat upheld the set-aside at §5.)
- NEVER FED: the builder's confidence prose · prior attempts' scores (each build is priced on its own record).
- Ceiling: ≤32k tokens; the HTML enters as its CONFIG + construction sections, not media.

## 4. Output space
- Verdict classes: **FIRE-WAVE · HOLD**.
- Dimensions (0–1): parameterization · purity pre-check · own-checks completeness · fidelity-plan honesty.
- Rejection taxonomy (one primary): HARDCODED-CONFIG · TRACING-STEP · ASSERTED-CHECKS (results claimed, not recorded) · SILENT-DEFERRAL.
- Accepted profile travels: cited in scores.md's pre-wave line; a wave fired over a HOLD is a deviation (WAVE-OVER-HOLD).

## 5. Floors and disposition
- Any dimension < 0.5 → HOLD · ASSERTED-CHECKS at any score on other dimensions → HOLD (unrecorded checks are the lane's named failure class).
- Disposition: the verdict is advisory — reviewed by the runner, applied or set aside with the miss recorded in Condense. The verdict + classification + disposition land in the build record either way; an applied HOLD leaves the item IN PROGRESS and the wave moves to its next item (a held item never holds the wave hostage).

## Call command
- BINDING OWED AT FIRST FIRING — the call fires in this job's next live cycle (owner's ruling 2026-11-14: arming = firing); binding it to the fleet's decision-model runner and recording the exact command here is the first firing's own first step, and the raw response files in the run record.

## 6. Firing record and improvement
- Exemplars: T15 attempt 1's build record (must FIRE-WAVE: checks recorded, deferral of the interlude named in its Plan) · a constructed record claiming a swap test with no harness output (must HOLD as ASSERTED-CHECKS) · a constructed record whose CONFIG is decorative over hard-coded phrases (must HOLD as HARDCODED-CONFIG).
- Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle; its firing record and misses are its improvement material. No test precedes firing. The exemplars above stand as its first firing cases — all three must classify correctly when fired, and a miss is recorded in Condense as improvement material, never as a bar to firing.

## 7. Iteration (Condense)
Amendments name forcing evidence + before/after. A text change takes effect on the call's next firing; the call keeps firing every cycle, and Condense prices the change against the returns that follow it.

## Owner's ruling addendum (2026-11-14 ~20:55 EST — supersedes gate phrasing above)
ARMING = FIRING: this call fires unconditionally in its owning job's next live cycle; the raw response files in the run record. There is no BUILT-NOT-FIRED state and no calibration gate — the calibration exemplars above are improvement material the firing record tests, not a precondition. VERDICTS ARE ADVISORY: applied, or set aside with the miss recorded in Condense + the call-file amendment it teaches. Any sentence above phrasing this call as blocking, gating, or preconditioning a step reads superseded by this addendum; the FIRING is the only non-skippable part.

## §9 — Condense
**Pattern (the wave's headline, §9 rule fired):** Amendment A13 — landed last cycle precisely to cure receipt incompleteness — met its first full wave, and **zero of five items cleared jev-prewave**. Every builder REPORTED its mechanical checks clean (harness assertions all PASS on all five final builds; A6 legs reported at 0 HITs on both legs for three items — T204 · T205 · T210 — with carried-with-disposition HITs on T199's fit leg and T208's census leg) — and three of those clean summaries were themselves found overstated by the re-fire returns (T204's fit row · T205's census rows · T205's CP01 PASS-at-0 ms against its failing 570 ms endpoint; §5 carries the itemized corrections) and every HOLD was ASSERTED-CHECKS on receipt grounds (the returns' own checking basis varies and is qualified in §9b.4 — T204/T210 are paperwork reviews; T205's re-fire independently evaluated its CP01 predicate and found the build failing the written strict bound, a stronger finding than a missing receipt): T199 (two index-line gaps in the fed paperwork, per its return's findings 1–2 and Limits: A-8's claimed probe inside the plate-owned window [0,800) has no filed dump in that window — the earliest filed dump is at 800 ms · B-17's swap receipt prints scale without the observed color the line claimed) · T204 (endpoint receipts + swap moved-instant receipts for five checkpoints + a fit row filed as a product instead of a measured bbox) · T205 (a strict-bound receipt failing at exactly 0.30 with no receipt at that instant · a fixed "8000ms" label in place of an evaluated instant · census rows overstating two phases' runtime effect) · T208 (timing-swap movement evidence for seven checkpoints via the inputs each assertion actually reads · a guard-#2 geometry receipt under content swap) · T210 (a hold-dependent swap probe · one stale index line printing a superseded value against its own receipt · CP9 without a whole-state equality receipt · palette swap without observed output values). The prewave own-checks dimension moved with cure rounds, stated by item (firing 1 → firing 2): T204 0.35→0.40 · T205 0.25→0.40 · T208 0.40→0.48 · T210 0.35→0.45 · T199 0.40→0.70 across cycles (Wave-33 firing 2 → this completion re-fire) — but never crossed. The defect is no longer enumeration (A13's index exists on every item and reconciliations were performed) — it is **receipt validity at the margin**: receipts that are present, plausible, and one predicate short of what their assertion prescribes, plus index lines whose claims outrun their receipts. On this wave's nine prewave firings, every prewave return identified receipt defects sufficient for HOLD on its stated checking basis; the builders' self-reconciliation did not. WHY the self-reconciliation missed them is **not established by this wave's evidence** — shared authorship (the same author writes the claim and checks it) is one hypothesis consistent with the filings, priced as a hypothesis in the candidate below, not as the pattern's cause.

**Candidate amendment (filed, NOT landed — priced for HQ): RECEIPT-INDEPENDENCE.** The A13 reconciliation gains a second pair of eyes: before the prewave payload is assembled, the receipt index is reconciled by a checker that did not author the build record — in this seat's form, a mechanical cross-check script where the assertion is mechanical (every CONFIG-expression receipt re-evaluated by script and diffed against the filed value; every claimed probe instant grepped in the filed raw output) plus the seat's spot re-probe of the index's highest-risk lines (swap receipts + endpoint receipts — swap-related receipt defects are named in all five of this wave's final HOLD returns, T205's missing moved CP10/CP11 probes included). Evidence: 5 items, one wave, one defect class, against A13's own prediction (Cycle 036 §0b.1: the index "is priced to cut the re-fire count" — the observed result this wave: four first attempts each used a re-fire, T199 used its carried completion re-fire, and none cleared; with one wave and no clearance cohort, the size of any A13 effect on re-fire frequency remains unestablished). Landing form (instruction step · mechanical guard · Jev definition · Codex prompt) is HQ's pricing; this cycle files the candidate with its evidence and does not amend INSTRUCTION.md, because a second consecutive self-authored amendment to the same leg, landed the cycle after A13's first full wave produced zero clearances (its effect on re-fire frequency unestablished, its failure mode not yet causally priced), is exactly the calibration-instead-of-pricing pattern the judgment-layer evaluation exists to catch. NOT-YET-AMENDED, consumer HQ.

The owner heard the four Codex returns verbatim, converged with the wave on the frozen-flow defect, and caught TWO defects no lens named. Both are standing law now:
1. **OWNER CURE 1 — foreground/background intensity separation (readability):** particles forming the word render FOREGROUND (contrast/opacity/intensity separated); reserve particles render BACKGROUND (dimmer). Same-intensity fields make formed words unreadable — in the original, the separation is part of why the words read at all. Any particle-field recreation carries this separation by construction.
2. **OWNER CURE 2 — strict phrase sequencing (phase exclusivity):** a phrase's formation may not begin until the previous phrase's dissolve has completed; one word's lifecycle owns the stage at a time. Overlapping lifecycles are a defect class in any sequenced recreation.
3. **THE FIFTH LENS — READABILITY (permanent, §5.4):** the founding evidence is priced: four lenses scored a recreation whose words fight their own background, and none owned legibility. The owner's expectation now has a judge (the golden rule). Attempt 2 onward is scored by five lenses; the bar form is unchanged (average ≥85, no lens <70) with the fifth lens inside both terms. Attempts scored under the four-lens wave keep their filed averages — cross-wave comparisons print the lens count (a 4-lens 78.5 and a 5-lens score are different instruments; deltas across the boundary are labeled as such).
4. **Flow-field construction model (HQ's implementation note, standing guidance for particle techniques):** model the pool as ONE constant flow field, not per-phase animations — every particle always carries the stream's directional velocity; forming = BORROWING particles out of the flow onto targets, where they keep low-amplitude jitter (never zero velocity), with a guaranteed reserve fraction that is never borrowed; dissolving = RELEASING them back to full stream velocity with continuity (no velocity replacement at phase boundaries). The intensity separation rides the borrow flag: borrowed = foreground, reserve = background. This single model carries Owner Cure 1, Owner Cure 2's clean stage handover, and attempt 1's convergent defect (frozen surplus) at once.

## §9 — Condense
Read the run's traces + Codex returns → candidate lessons (which constructions scored well on which lenses; recurring Codex findings across techniques) → label demonstrated/candidate/planned → promote into this file (dated, technique cited) + verdict-history.md → next cycle inherits. A lens finding that recurs across two techniques is a pattern: it becomes a §6 guard candidate with the two citations.
- **Condense Codex call point:** `guards/codex-condense.md` fires at Condense close — it attacks the cycle's drafted amendments for faithfulness (does the amended text encode the lesson) and four-layer coverage (steps · mechanical guards · Jev definitions · Codex prompts; each untouched layer carries a NO-DEFECT proof or a named not-yet-amended reason). A REVISE returns the named amendment for re-encoding before the cycle record closes. Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle; a cycle in which it did not fire prints INCOMPLETE. (Built 2026-11-14.)

## §10 — Delivery / reporting
- Completion reported on this job's HQ.json + lane heartbeat: technique, attempt #, five lens scores + average, verdict, artifact path.

## Amendment A13 (2026-11-18, Cycle 036 Condense — the receipt-completeness pattern: T190 · T196 · T197 · T199 prewave HOLDs in one wave; T192's 1.0-clearance as the positive demonstration; re-encoded under codex-condense REVISE before landing)
1. **Receipt index + reconciliation (operative step: §5 leg 3b's readiness check, extended in the same landing).** Every build-record carries a RECEIPT INDEX enumerating (a) every guards.md command applicable to the item (1 purity · 2 swap · 3 syntax + seeded-RNG census · 4 arithmetic, seat-side · 5 segment binding · 6 fit leg · 7 census leg · 8 census-anchor probes where a zero-state anchor stands) and (b) every A11 assertion for the item. Readiness REQUIRES the reconciliation, not just the index: against the applicable guard commands, the item's planned A11 assertions, and — for a completion or re-attempt — the assertion sets established in the filed prior harnesses and their amendments; census receipts reconcile against the build's expected CONFIG keys (nested/array keys included — founding miss: T190's `scenes[].id` row). Every line carries its executable receipt or an explicit NOT-DEMANDED line whose disposition cites the governing plan or guard; a missing expected line, an unsupported exclusion, or a line with neither is incomplete at the builder's own gate: jev-prewave is not spent on it (the firing obligation is unchanged — the readiness check precedes payload assembly, per A12.1's form).
2. **Assertion inheritance.** A completion/re-attempt builder inherits the prior attempt's assertion set WHOLE (every checkpoint the prior harness evaluated, by name); an assertion leaves the set only by a printed disposition in the new build-record, tied to the governing plan or guard. A re-derived set that silently drops an assertion is a builder defect printed in the record (founding instance: T197 Cycle 036, CP4-`or`).
3. **Receipt validity (evidence form, not presence).** A receipt is valid only if it carries the evidence its assertion requires: for each CONFIG-derived input, the CONFIG expression and its evaluated value (never a bare number whose CONFIG dependence the judge must take on faith — founding instances: T196's boundary-skip endpoints · T199's census twins); for each check, the raw observed result. Item-specific evidence stands under its own assertion (founding instance: T199's joined CP11 receipt stands under CP11, not as an appendix claim). A populated receipt lacking its required evidence returns the record to the builder under §5 leg 3b's readiness check.
# Guards — recreation

## The judgment layer's home (owner's order, 2026-11-14 — 100% form)
Form: **In-job form.** This job's judging = the Codex scoring wave (its review type, defined in `codex-scoring-wave.md` in this directory) + the mechanical guards below + `jev-prewave.md`. The wave's execution may be shared tooling; the definitions are this job's own files.

## The commands (mechanical guards — callable, demonstrated)
1. Construction-purity grep — recreation.html must contain no reference to source media, frames, or tracing (pattern census over the file).
   Demonstrated: 2026-11-13, T15 attempt 1 (zero hits; Codex purity lens independently returned 100).
   Pattern precision (2026-11-17, Cycle 026 Condense — priced across T167 · T170 · T175 in one wave): an in-file fragment reference `url(#id)` (an SVG paint-server or clip definition inside the same file) is NOT a specimen reference; such hits are classified in the item's build record and never counted as purity hits. The refusal class is specimen media, source files, and tracing — the grep's specimen alternatives (`segment.mp4|source.mp4|<video|<img|1235931636`-form source ids) stay unmodified.
2. Swap test (parameterization proof) — headless harness loads the recreation with a swapped CONFIG phrase; target geometry must regenerate for the new words (a traced copy cannot).
   Demonstrated: 2026-11-13, T15 attempt 1, LOGIC level (node harness, stubbed canvas: 5,060 → 2,728 targets). Pixel-level render proof is NOT exercisable from this seat (no browser binary) — printed limitation, carried in scores.md; the comparison page is the render surface.
3. Syntax + determinism check — `node --check` on inline scripts; seeded-RNG presence census.
   Demonstrated: 2026-11-13, T15 attempt 1 (both scripts clean).
4. Scores arithmetic — recompute the wave average + min from the filed lens returns before scores.md prints a verdict.
   Demonstrated: 2026-11-13, T15 attempt 1 (68/82/100/64 → 78.5 recomputed at filing).
5. Segment binding — segment.mp4's timestamps/duration/ffprobe facts recorded against the packet's technique window before any build starts.
   Demonstrated: 2026-11-13, T15 attempt 1 (1:24–1:40 · 16.0 s · ffprobe-cited).
6. **Measured-fit leg** — `guard-measured-fit.md` in this directory. Per item, pre-wave: every subject text element carries a MEASURED fit row (post-fit ink bounds vs container at the 360 px stage, legibility floor ~11 px); a formula-claimed fit or a missing row is a HIT. Owns the "fit asserted, not measured" readability class (Waves 2–5, 10). **Built 2026-11-15 (HQ owed-checks charge); fires unconditionally at the pre-wave step from the next cycle; a wave record lacking a fit table for any item prints INCOMPLETE.** Verdict advisory; firing is not.
7. **Config-census leg (v3.1 codified)** — `guard-config-census.md` in this directory. Per item, pre-wave: all-literal scan with per-constant subject-shaping/structural classification + CONFIG key→application-site resolution (nested/array keys included) + CSS-variable setter check; unapplied keys and unset-variable references are HITs. Owns the config-incompleteness reusability class. **Built 2026-11-15 (HQ owed-checks charge); fires unconditionally at the pre-wave step from the next cycle; a wave record lacking a census for any item prints INCOMPLETE.** Verdict advisory; firing is not.

## Jev call definitions
- `jev-prewave.md` — judges a build record's readiness to spend a scoring wave. **Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle. A cycle in which it did not fire prints INCOMPLETE.** (Designed 2026-11-14; attempt 1 predates it and ran under the §5 panel, which runs alongside.)
- `jev-observe-exit.md` — the Observe→Plan gate for a recreation wave (queue state priced · prior/family scores read · mechanics seen · variables extracted · flow-field model + Owner Cures 1–2 carried in working memory · consumer named). Call point: INSTRUCTION §1 gate out. **Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle. A cycle in which it did not fire prints INCOMPLETE.** (Phase-guard augmentation, 2026-11-14.)
- `jev-plan-exit.md` — the Plan→Execute gate (parameter table · Execute + Verify checklists as instruments · failure points priced · plateau floor rule priced in Plan). Call point: INSTRUCTION §2 gate out. **Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle. A cycle in which it did not fire prints INCOMPLETE.** (2026-11-14.)
- Deliverable Jev: **none separate, by design** — the deliverable class (a scored recreation attempt) is judged by the five-lens Codex scoring wave + §5.5 consolidation; a jev-verify file would duplicate the wave. jev-prewave is its pre-wave gate. (Printed in INSTRUCTION §5 step 3b.)

- `codex-scoring-wave.md` — the five-lens scoring wave (motion fidelity · timing/tempo · construction purity · reusability · readability). Call point: INSTRUCTION §2 gate out, alongside jev-plan-exit. **Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle. A cycle in which it did not fire prints INCOMPLETE.** (2026-11-14.)
- `codex-condense.md` — Condense review: amendment faithfulness + four-layer coverage attack. Call point: INSTRUCTION §9, at Condense close. Consumer: the job's next cycle. **Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle. A cycle in which it did not fire prints INCOMPLETE.** (2026-11-14.)

     1	# Codex review — scoring wave (recreation)
     2	
     3	_Repeated Codex review type, defined in this job's own guards layer (owner's 100% order, 2026-11-14). Execution: Codex via the fleet feeding rule; the DEFINITION lives here._
     4	
     5	## The review
     6	Five independent lens runs score ONE recreation attempt against the BAR: **average of the five lens scores ≥ 85 AND no lens < 70** (the recreation law; a pass at this bar is the only route to RECREATED).
     7	Lenses: **motion fidelity** (does the recreation's movement equal the technique's described movement?) · **timing/tempo** (phase timings vs the original's measured window) · **construction purity** (parameterized construction vs tracing/embedding — a refusal-class judgment) · **reusability** (could production drive it from CONFIG alone for a different piece?) · **readability** (owner's fifth lens, 2026-11-14: formed words legible against their own particle field — intensity separation, phone-size legibility during hold, background never at word intensity).
     8	
     9	## Feeding contract
    10	- ONE stream per lens run: lens instruction file + the artifacts it judges, concatenated — never pipe + stdin together (fleet rule; a doubled stream voids the run).
    11	- **Score-blind allowed packet (ADOPTED 2026-11-14, Cycle 009 Condense — the Wave-6 adoption decision executed):** lens payloads are the ALLOWED PACKET only (lens instruction · neutral technique facts · the attempt's recreation.html · a score-blind build record). scores.md is NEVER fed to a lens; no lens sees a prior score or another lens's return.
    12	- **Identical-hash binding (ADOPTED 2026-11-14, Cycle 009):** all five returns of an attempt must bind to the identical final recreation.html SHA-256. When a lens catches an interim-build defect, the build is cured, self-verify re-runs, and the wave re-fires on the final build; the interim return is preserved verbatim (e.g. `codex/lens-motion-interim1.md`) and never enters the final average. Founding instance: Wave 6, T38 (interim motion 35 → cured → five lenses re-fired on the final build).
    13	- **Every-firing preservation duty (ADOPTED 2026-11-14, Cycle 009 — practice A-C003-1 generalized):** every firing's payload + return is filed create-once in the attempt directory BEFORE any subsequent source change, payload regeneration, or re-fire, for every attempt number; a discarded interim return files as a printed deviation with the material recorded UNAVAILABLE.
    14	- Every lens instruction opens with the standing header: (1) consumer = recreation job's §5 step + the decision priced (tier movement / cure list), (2) the working record it builds from and writes to (the artifact's codex/ directory + scores.md), (3) the return must end with `SCORE: <integer>` and a `BODY-WRITE:` line naming what it filed.
    15	- Lenses run independently; no lens sees another's return.
    16	- Returns file VERBATIM in the artifact's `codex/lens-<name>.md`; scores.md cites them; the seat recomputes average + min mechanically (guards.md command 4) — a wave average is never taken from a return's own arithmetic.
    17	
    18	## Consumer
    19	- Consumer: recreation's §5 Verify (tier recommendation) + the cycle's Condense (cure list, convergent-lens rule: a defect two lenses name becomes the next attempt's primary cure) + practice (the filed returns are its Observe input).
    20	- Decision priced: whether this attempt is RECREATED-tier, and exactly what the next attempt must cure.
    21	
    22	## Demonstration state
    23	- Four-lens form: demonstrated 2026-11-13, T15 attempt 1 (68/82/100/64 → 78.5, FAIL, returns on file).
    24	- Five-lens form (readability born 2026-11-14): demonstrated Waves 1–6 (2026-11-14, 25 attempts) under the score-blind + hash-binding practice adopted at Wave 6.
    25	
    26	## Adoption record (2026-11-14, Cycle 009 Condense — replaces the divergence notices printed here 2026-11-14)
    27	Recreation's Wave-6 Condense adopted the practice-copy rules (practice Cycle-002 A-C002-4 + payload/binding corrections, and Cycle-003 A-C003-1's every-firing preservation duty) after applying and proving them in Wave 6, and charged the text amendment to the next Condense. That amendment lands here at Cycle 009: the three rules now stand in the Feeding contract above as this job's own text. The divergence between this file and practice's `codex-rescore-wave.md` is CLOSED on these three rules; the historical notices are retired by this record, not silently deleted (Wave-6 RUN.md Condense + this adoption record carry the history).
    28	
    29	## Amendment — census-anchor mapping is a required timing-payload element (2026-11-16, recreation Cycle 025 Condense)
    30	From Wave 23 onward, the **timing lens payload** for every item MUST include the item's **census-anchor mapping table** (window-start offset · each census state's segment-relative ms · the build's prescribed state at that instant, per the jev-plan-exit consistency leg landed the same Condense). A timing payload without the mapping is a feeder defect, printed as such in the item's build record; the lens prices the schedule against the mapping, not against the builder's summary of it. Forcing evidence: T160 Wave 22 timing 68 + T162 Wave 22 timing 64 (filed returns: the items' `codex/lens-timing.md`) — in both, the schedule the lens was asked to judge contradicted the census anchors the Plan claimed to bind, and the mapping that would have exposed the contradiction at Plan time existed nowhere as a single table.
    31

The divergence is practice-only as of this notice; adoption here is recreation's own Condense decision.**

../../guards/jev-observe-exit.md:7:- Call point: recreation INSTRUCTION §1, at the gate out of Observe, before §2 Plan opens for any item in the wave. The verdict is advisory per item: applied, a RETURN-TO-OBSERVE item is held out of the wave's Plan (the wave proceeds with its advancing items; a held item never holds the wave hostage — same rule as jev-prewave §5); set aside, the miss is recorded in Condense. An unfired call makes the cycle INCOMPLETE.
../../guards/jev-observe-exit.md:28:- Rejection taxonomy (one primary per returned item): QUEUE-STALE (queue position/status not read this cycle) · REATTEMPT-MISROUTED (scored technique in a first-attempt wave) · SCORES-UNREAD (prior scores/cures exist, uncited) · MECHANICS-UNSEEN (no firsthand sub-beat record) · VARIABLES-THIN (variable list cannot bind a parameter table) · GUIDANCE-DROPPED (standing family guidance absent from working memory) · CONSUMER-VAGUE.
../../guards/jev-observe-exit.md:37:- Defined prompt + context assembly per §3, executed through the fleet's sealed decision-model route named in guards.md when bound. Binding the exact runner command here is the first firing's own first step. **Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle. A cycle in which it did not fire prints INCOMPLETE.**
../../INSTRUCTION.md:23:- **Codex call point (Observe):** `guards/codex-observe.md` fires per item at §1 step 1 (source-record audit) on every cycle. Its receipts persist in RUN.md's Observe section; a cycle in which it did not fire prints INCOMPLETE.
../../INSTRUCTION.md:24:- **Observe-exit call:** `guards/jev-observe-exit.md` fires at this gate on every cycle and judges the gate-out block per item before §2 opens for that item. The phase advances on the verdict's recorded disposition (applied, or set aside with reason; applied gaps are carried into §2 as named charges). An unfired call makes the cycle INCOMPLETE.
../../INSTRUCTION.md:33:- **Plan-exit call:** `guards/jev-plan-exit.md` fires at this gate on every cycle and judges the Plan product per item (parameter table · checklists · failure points · plateau priced in Plan per Amendment A2 — prior/family average + the stuck-lens hypothesis stated before Execute, not discovered at close) before §3 opens. `guards/codex-plan-review.md` fires alongside it for every item. Both verdicts are advisory: the phase advances on their recorded dispositions (applied, or set aside with reason). An unfired call makes the cycle INCOMPLETE.
../../INSTRUCTION.md:49:3b. **Pre-wave Jev gate:** `guards/jev-prewave.md` judges the build record after legs 1–3 and before the wave (leg 4): FIRE-WAVE spends the wave; HOLD returns the build to Execute with its classification and the wave moves to its next item. **Receipt readiness check (Amendment A12, 2026-11-18 — performed by the builder BEFORE the payload is assembled):** every A11 checkpoint in the build-record carries its executable receipt — the harness command or run, the CONFIG expression evaluated, and the raw result from the build under review; for a CONFIG timing swap the receipt shows the changed evaluation instant. A checkpoint with a claim but no receipt returns the record to the builder before the call is spent. **Receipt-index reconciliation (Amendment A13, 2026-11-18):** the build-record carries a RECEIPT INDEX enumerating (a) every guards.md command applicable to the item and (b) every A11 assertion for the item; before the payload is assembled the builder reconciles the index against the applicable guard commands, the item's planned A11 assertions, and — for a completion or re-attempt — the assertion sets established in the filed prior harnesses and their amendments, and, for census receipts, against the build's expected CONFIG keys (nested/array keys included). Every index line carries its executable receipt or an explicit NOT-DEMANDED line whose disposition cites the governing plan or guard; a missing expected line, an unsupported exclusion, or a line with neither returns the record to the builder. A receipt is valid only if it carries the evidence its assertion requires: for each CONFIG-derived input, the CONFIG expression and its evaluated value; for each check, the raw observed result. A populated receipt lacking that evidence returns the record to the builder on the same form. This check is a readiness check for the mandatory call, not a gate on it: the call still fires at its step on every cycle, and a cycle in which it did not fire prints INCOMPLETE under the standing firing ruling. The deliverable-class judgment for this job IS the five-lens scoring wave (leg 4, defined in `guards/codex-scoring-wave.md`) — no separate jev-verify file exists, by design: the wave's per-lens verdicts + leg 5's mechanical consolidation are the deliverable gate, and a second Jev over the same deliverable would duplicate them.
../../INSTRUCTION.md:89:- **Condense Codex call point:** `guards/codex-condense.md` fires at Condense close — it attacks the cycle's drafted amendments for faithfulness (does the amended text encode the lesson) and four-layer coverage (steps · mechanical guards · Jev definitions · Codex prompts; each untouched layer carries a NO-DEFECT proof or a named not-yet-amended reason). A REVISE returns the named amendment for re-encoding before the cycle record closes. Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle; a cycle in which it did not fire prints INCOMPLETE. (Built 2026-11-14.)
../../INSTRUCTION.md:96:Before this job's Condense closes, every cycle, evaluate the judgment layer AS HOSTED IN THIS JOB'S OWN HOME (the `guards/` directory at the home root): (a) every judge this job's deliverables demand is present as its own file there — Jev calls (instruction + call command), deterministic guards, Codex review instructions for the review types this job repeats; (b) each is current against this cycle's experience (a verdict reversed, a defect that passed, a review that missed — each either amended its file in this Condense or is named in the cycle record as not-yet-amended with its reason); (c) any judge the deliverable demanded this cycle that does not exist yet is CREATED in this step or carried as a named build charge with pilot sequencing — never noted-and-dropped. The verdict prints in the cycle record: **JUDGMENT LAYER: COMPLETE** (all demanded judges hosted + current) or **INCOMPLETE** (missing/stale judges named). INCOMPLETE is a lawful print; a silent Condense is not. JOB-05's census reads this field fleet-wide.
../../INSTRUCTION.md:97:Founding print (2026-11-14): JUDGMENT LAYER: **INCOMPLETE** — mechanical guards hosted + demonstrated (5/5); Codex scoring wave defined in-job and demonstrated in four-lens form (five-lens form demonstrates at its first wave); jev-prewave designed, **UNBUILT** — build charge carried in this job's Condense until armed.
../../INSTRUCTION.md:99:**Ruling addendum (2026-11-14 ~20:55 EST — owner's judgment-call ruling):** arming = firing. This job's Jev/Codex steps are non-skippable and fire unconditionally in its next live cycle, raw responses filed in the run record; verdicts are advisory — applied, or set aside with the miss recorded in Condense + the call-file amendment it teaches. The founding print above reads superseded where it phrases any call as gated, conditional, or UNBUILT-blocked; presence from the next cycle prices whether the prescribed firings HAPPENED (a cycle closing without one is INCOMPLETE).
../../INSTRUCTION.md:102:The measured-fit leg and the config-census leg — candidacy text since Waves 5–6 — are built as standing guards: `guards/guard-measured-fit.md` and `guards/guard-config-census.md` (v3.1 codified, including application-site resolution for nested/array keys and the CSS-variable setter check). **Pre-wave step (non-skippable):** for every item in a wave, after the build and BEFORE the five-lens scoring wave fires, both legs run on the item's final recreation.html and their outputs file in the item's artifact directory (`fit-table.md`, `config-census.md`); the wave RUN cites each leg's path + hit count per item. A wave record lacking either leg's output for any item prints **INCOMPLETE**. Leg verdicts are advisory (owner's 2026-11-14 ruling): hits are cured pre-scoring where the builder can, or carried into the wave printed with their disposition; misses (a leg's false hit or missed defect) are recorded in Condense with the guard-file amendment they teach. Scope: the legs own the fit and config classes only — timing anchors, density judgment, and content failures are NOT theirs and are never presented as cured by them.
../../INSTRUCTION.md:109:1. **Plate construction is a Plan requirement (A9.1).** Any Plan adopting a moving background plate prices its construction in the parameter table: for techniques where a moving plate IS the technique (the RSVP family form), the plate is a seamless tiled pan — repeat period = pan wrap, pan clock continuous across cycle wrap, and **tiles endpoint-periodic** (a tile whose end state differs from its start state steps at the join — T139 motion lens, Cycle 023). For a case-only stream technique (T137's form), the Plan prices a still plate — any background motion exceeds the technique.
../../INSTRUCTION.md:126:1. **Receipt form (operative step: §5 leg 3b's readiness check, referenced from §3 step 4).** Every A11 assertion in a build-record files its EXECUTABLE RECEIPT beside the claim — the harness command or run, the CONFIG expression evaluated, and the raw result from the build under review; for a CONFIG timing swap the receipt shows the changed evaluation instant. A9.4 already required raw output; this clause names the receipt's three parts and wires the check into the prewave step itself. A build-record whose assertions carry claims without receipts is incomplete at the builder's own gate: jev-prewave is not spent on it (the call's firing obligation is unchanged — the readiness check precedes payload assembly, and an unfired call still prints the cycle INCOMPLETE).
